function _pick(pack, ...)
    for i = 1, select("#", ...) do
        local v = pack[select(i, ...)]
        if v ~= nil then return v end
    end
    return nil
end

_unwalkConn = nil
customAnimApplyToken = 0

function _setAnim(animObj, id)
    if not animObj or not id then return end
    pcall(function()
        animObj.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
    end)
end

function _ensureAnim(folder, name)
    if not folder then return nil end
    local a = folder:FindFirstChild(name)
    if not a then
        a = Instance.new("Animation")
        a.Name = name
        a.Parent = folder
    end
    return a
end

function _ensureFolder(animate, folderName)
    local f = animate:FindFirstChild(folderName)
    if not f then
        f = Instance.new("Folder")
        f.Name = folderName
        f.Parent = animate
    end
    return f
end

function waitForAnimate(char)
    if not char then return nil end
    for _ = 1, 60 do
        if not char.Parent then return nil end
        local a = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if a and a:FindFirstChild("walk") and a:FindFirstChild("run") then
            local walk = a:FindFirstChild("walk")
            local run = a:FindFirstChild("run")
            if walk and run then return a end
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    return char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
end

applyAnimPack = function(packName)
    if not Config.CustomAnim then return end
    Config.Unwalk = false
    if type(stopUnwalk) == "function" then

        if _unwalkConn then pcall(function() _unwalkConn:Disconnect() end); _unwalkConn = nil end
    end
    packName = packName or Config.AnimPack
    local pack = ANIM_PACKS[packName]
    if not pack then

        for k, v in pairs(ANIM_PACKS) do
            if string.lower(k) == string.lower(tostring(packName)) then
                pack = v
                packName = k
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if not pack then return end
    Config.AnimPack = packName

    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return end
    local animate = waitForAnimate(char)
    if not animate then return end

    snapshotCharacterAnimations(char)
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        for _, t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end)
        end
    end

    local runObj = _ensureAnim(_ensureFolder(animate, "run"), "RunAnim")
    local walkObj = _ensureAnim(_ensureFolder(animate, "walk"), "WalkAnim")
    local jumpObj = _ensureAnim(_ensureFolder(animate, "jump"), "JumpAnim")
    local fallObj = _ensureAnim(_ensureFolder(animate, "fall"), "FallAnim")
    local climbObj = _ensureAnim(_ensureFolder(animate, "climb"), "ClimbAnim")
    local swimObj = _ensureAnim(_ensureFolder(animate, "swim"), "Swim")
    local swimIdleObj = _ensureAnim(_ensureFolder(animate, "swimidle"), "SwimIdle")
    local idleFolder = _ensureFolder(animate, "idle")

    _setAnim(walkObj, _pick(pack, "WalkAnim", "Walk"))
    _setAnim(runObj, _pick(pack, "RunAnim", "Run"))
    _setAnim(jumpObj, _pick(pack, "JumpAnim", "Jump"))
    _setAnim(fallObj, _pick(pack, "FallAnim", "Fall"))
    _setAnim(climbObj, _pick(pack, "ClimbAnim", "Climb"))
    _setAnim(swimObj, _pick(pack, "Swim"))
    _setAnim(swimIdleObj, _pick(pack, "SwimIdle") or _pick(pack, "Swim"))

    if idleFolder then
        local a1 = _pick(pack, "Animation1")
        local a2 = _pick(pack, "Animation2")
        if a1 or a2 then
            _setAnim(_ensureAnim(idleFolder, "Animation1"), a1 or a2)
            _setAnim(_ensureAnim(idleFolder, "Animation2"), a2 or a1)
        elseif pack.Idle then
            _setAnim(_ensureAnim(idleFolder, "Animation1"), pack.Idle[1])
            _setAnim(_ensureAnim(idleFolder, "Animation2"), pack.Idle[2] or pack.Idle[1])
        end
    end

    animate.Disabled = true
    task.wait(0.06)
    animate.Disabled = false

    if hum then
        pcall(function()
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed)
            task.wait(0.03)
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        end)
    end
end

function startUnwalk()
    Config.Unwalk = true
    Config.CustomAnim = false
    if _unwalkConn then pcall(function() _unwalkConn:Disconnect() end); _unwalkConn = nil end
    local function stopTracks(char)
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            for _, t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end)
            end
        end
        local animate = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if animate then
            pcall(function() animate.Disabled = true end)
        end
    end
    local char = LP.Character
    if char then
        pcall(function() snapshotCharacterAnimations(char) end)
        stopTracks(char)
    end

    _unwalkConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not Config.Unwalk then return end
        local c = LP.Character
        if not c then return end
        local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end
        for _, t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end)
        end
        local animate = c:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if animate and not animate.Disabled then
            pcall(function() animate.Disabled = true end)
        end
    end)
end

function stopUnwalk()
    Config.Unwalk = false
    if _unwalkConn then pcall(function() _unwalkConn:Disconnect() end); _unwalkConn = nil end
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return end
    local animate = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if animate then pcall(function() animate.Disabled = false end) end
    if Config.CustomAnim then
        pcall(function() applyAnimPack(Config.AnimPack) end)
    else
        pcall(function() restoreCharacterAnimations(char) end)
    end
end

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    customAnimApplyToken = (customAnimApplyToken or 0) + 1
    local applyToken = customAnimApplyToken
    if Config.Unwalk then
        task.wait(0.5)
        if Config.Unwalk then pcall(startUnwalk) end
    elseif Config.CustomAnim then
        task.spawn(function()
            for _, delayTime in ipairs({0.35, 0.8, 1.4}) do
                task.wait(delayTime)
                if applyToken ~= customAnimApplyToken or not Config.CustomAnim or LP.Character ~= char then return end
                if applyAnimPack then pcall(applyAnimPack, Config.AnimPack) end
            end
        end)
    else
        task.wait(0.15)
        pcall(function()
            local animate = char and char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
            if animate then animate.Disabled = false end
        end)
    end
end)


antiLagState = {
    conn = nil,
    active = false,
    cache = {},
    lighting = nil,
    effects = {},
    beauty = {},
}

function peakCacheProperty(instance, property)
    local entry = antiLagState.cache[instance]
    if not entry then entry = {}; antiLagState.cache[instance] = entry end
    if entry[property] == nil then
        local ok, value = pcall(function() return instance[property] end)
        if ok then entry[property] = value end
    end
end

function _applyAntiLagObj(instance)
    if not antiLagState.active then return end
    pcall(function()
        if instance:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or instance:IsA("Trail") or instance:IsA("Beam")
            or instance:IsA("Fire") or instance:IsA("Smoke") or instance:IsA("Sparkles") then
            peakCacheProperty(instance, "Enabled")
            instance.Enabled = false
        elseif instance:IsA("BasePart") then
            peakCacheProperty(instance, "CastShadow")
            instance.CastShadow = false
            if instance.Material == Enum.Material.Glass or instance.Material == Enum.Material.ForceField then
                peakCacheProperty(instance, "Material")
                peakCacheProperty(instance, "Reflectance")
                instance.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                instance.Reflectance = 0
            end
        end
    end)
end

function peakClearBeautyEffects()
    for _, obj in pairs(antiLagState.beauty) do
        pcall(function()
            if obj and obj.Parent then obj:Destroy() end
        end)
    end
    table.clear(antiLagState.beauty)
end

function peakApplyEmotionalGraphics()
    peakClearBeautyEffects()

    local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
    cc.Name = "PeakHubEmotionCC"
    cc.Brightness = 0.06
    cc.Contrast = 0.18
    cc.Saturation = 0.22
    cc.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 228, 242)
    cc.Parent = Lighting
    antiLagState.beauty.cc = cc

    local bloom = Instance.new("BloomEffect")
    bloom.Name = "PeakHubEmotionBloom"
    bloom.Intensity = 0.55
    bloom.Size = 28
    bloom.Threshold = 0.92
    bloom.Parent = Lighting
    antiLagState.beauty.bloom = bloom

    local dof = Instance.new("DepthOfFieldEffect")
    dof.Name = "PeakHubEmotionDoF"
    dof.FarIntensity = 0.18
    dof.NearIntensity = 0.05
    dof.FocusDistance = 28
    dof.InFocusRadius = 22
    dof.Parent = Lighting
    antiLagState.beauty.dof = dof

    local rays = Instance.new("SunRaysEffect")
    rays.Name = "PeakHubEmotionRays"
    rays.Intensity = 0.08
    rays.Spread = 0.65
    rays.Parent = Lighting
    antiLagState.beauty.rays = rays

    local atmo = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
    if not atmo then
        atmo = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
        atmo.Name = "PeakHubEmotionAtmosphere"
        atmo.Parent = Lighting
        antiLagState.beauty.atmo = atmo
    else
        antiLagState.beauty.atmoSaved = {
            Density = atmo.Density,
            Offset = atmo.Offset,
            Color = atmo.Color,
            Decay = atmo.Decay,
            Glare = atmo.Glare,
            Haze = atmo.Haze,
        }
    end
    atmo.Density = 0.28
    atmo.Offset = 0.12
    atmo.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 210)
    atmo.Decay = Color3.fromRGB(120, 90, 160)
    atmo.Glare = 0.35
    atmo.Haze = 1.6

    Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(95, 70, 110)
    Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(140, 110, 150)
    Lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 230)
    Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.fromRGB(80, 60, 120)
    Lighting.Brightness = 2.15
    Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0.55
    Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0.35
    Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
    Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(200, 160, 190)
    Lighting.FogStart = 40
    Lighting.FogEnd = 520
    Lighting.ClockTime = 19.35
    Lighting.GeographicLatitude = 25

    task.spawn(function()
        local t0 = tick()
        while antiLagState.active do
            local t = tick() - t0
            local pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(t * 0.55)
            local warm = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 235)
            local cool = Color3.fromRGB(230, 210, 255)
            if antiLagState.beauty.cc and antiLagState.beauty.cc.Parent then
                antiLagState.beauty.cc.TintColor = warm:Lerp(cool, pulse * 0.45)
                antiLagState.beauty.cc.Brightness = 0.04 + pulse * 0.05
                antiLagState.beauty.cc.Saturation = 0.18 + pulse * 0.08
            end
            if antiLagState.beauty.bloom and antiLagState.beauty.bloom.Parent then
                antiLagState.beauty.bloom.Intensity = 0.42 + pulse * 0.22
            end
            task.wait(0.08)
        end
    end)
end

enableAntiLag = function()
    if antiLagState.active then return end
    antiLagState.active = true
    antiLagState.cache = {}
    antiLagState.lighting = {
        GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
        FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
        FogStart = Lighting.FogStart,
        FogColor = Lighting.FogColor,
        Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
        Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
        OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
        ColorShift_Top = Lighting.ColorShift_Top,
        ColorShift_Bottom = Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom,
        EnvironmentDiffuseScale = Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale,
        EnvironmentSpecularScale = Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale,
        ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime,
        GeographicLatitude = Lighting.GeographicLatitude,
    }
    for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("PostEffect") then
            antiLagState.effects[child] = child.Enabled
            pcall(function() child.Enabled = false end)
        end
    end

    peakApplyEmotionalGraphics()

    if antiLagState.conn then antiLagState.conn:Disconnect() end
    antiLagState.conn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
        if antiLagState.active then _applyAntiLagObj(descendant) end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        local descendants = workspace:GetDescendants()
        for i = 1, #descendants do
            if not antiLagState.active then break end
            _applyAntiLagObj(descendants[i])
            if i % 220 == 0 then task.wait() end
        end
    end)
end

disableAntiLag = function()
    antiLagState.active = false
    if antiLagState.conn then antiLagState.conn:Disconnect(); antiLagState.conn = nil end

    peakClearBeautyEffects()

    for instance, properties in pairs(antiLagState.cache) do
        if instance and instance.Parent then
            for property, value in pairs(properties) do
                pcall(function() instance[property] = value end)
            end
        end
    end
    table.clear(antiLagState.cache)

    for effect, enabled in pairs(antiLagState.effects) do
        if effect and effect.Parent then pcall(function() effect.Enabled = enabled end) end
    end
    table.clear(antiLagState.effects)

    if antiLagState.lighting then
        for property, value in pairs(antiLagState.lighting) do
            pcall(function() Lighting[property] = value end)
        end
        antiLagState.lighting = nil
    end

    local atmo = Lighting:FindFirstChild("PeakHubEmotionAtmosphere")
    if atmo then pcall(function() atmo:Destroy() end) end
    local saved = antiLagState.beauty and antiLagState.beauty.atmoSaved
    if saved then
        local existing = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
        if existing then
            for k, v in pairs(saved) do pcall(function() existing[k] = v end) end
        end
    end
    table.clear(antiLagState.beauty)
end


stretchRezConn = nil
stretchValue = 0.70
enableStretchRez = function()
    if stretchRezConn then stretchRezConn:Disconnect() end
    stretchRezConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not Config.StretchRes then
            if stretchRezConn then stretchRezConn:Disconnect(); stretchRezConn = nil end
            return
        end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then
            cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, stretchValue, 0, 0, 0, 1)
        end
    end)
end
disableStretchRez = function()
    if stretchRezConn then stretchRezConn:Disconnect(); stretchRezConn = nil end
end


customFovConn = nil
enableCustomFov = function()
    if customFovConn then customFovConn:Disconnect() end
    pcall(function() workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = Config.FOVValue end)
    customFovConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not Config.FOVChange then
            if customFovConn then customFovConn:Disconnect(); customFovConn = nil end
            return
        end
        pcall(function() workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = Config.FOVValue end)
    end)
end
disableCustomFov = function()
    if customFovConn then customFovConn:Disconnect(); customFovConn = nil end
    pcall(function() workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = 70 end)
end






overheadGui = nil
overheadDiscord = nil
overheadSpeed = nil
overheadRagdoll = nil
overheadConn = nil
overheadRagEnd = 0
OVERHEAD_PURPLE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 80)



function getOverheadBodyAnchor(char)
    if not char then return nil end
    local preferred = {
        "HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "Torso", "LowerTorso",
        "Head", "UpperTorso", "RightUpperTorso", "LeftUpperTorso",
        "RightUpperArm", "LeftUpperArm", "Right Arm", "Left Arm",
        "RightUpperLeg", "LeftUpperLeg", "Right Leg", "Left Leg",
    }
    for _, partName in ipairs(preferred) do
        local part = char:FindFirstChild(partName)
        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") and part:IsDescendantOf(char) then
            return part
        end
    end

    for _, item in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if item:IsA("BasePart") and item.Name ~= "Handle" then
            return item
        end
    end
    return nil
end

function ensureOverheadLabels(char)
    char = char or LP.Character
    if not char then return end
    local anchor = getOverheadBodyAnchor(char)
    if not anchor then return end

    if overheadGui and overheadGui.Parent then
        if overheadGui.Adornee ~= anchor then
            overheadGui.Adornee = anchor
        end
        if overheadGui.Parent ~= anchor then
            overheadGui.Parent = anchor
        end
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        if overheadGui then overheadGui:Destroy() end
    end)

    local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    bb.Name = "VyzeOverhead"
    bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 70)
    bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.4, 0)
    bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
    bb.MaxDistance = 200
    bb.Adornee = anchor
    bb.Parent = anchor
    overheadGui = bb

    bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 0, 90)
    bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.8, 0)

    local function makeLbl(y, size, text)
        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, size)
        lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = text
        lbl.TextColor3 = _G.PeakHubThemeAccent or OVERHEAD_PURPLE
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        lbl.TextSize = size
        lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.25
        lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        lbl.Parent = bb
        return lbl
    end


    overheadRagdoll = makeLbl(0, 18, "")
    overheadRagdoll.TextSize = 15
    overheadRagdoll.Visible = false

    overheadDiscord = makeLbl(20, 26, "discord.gg/peakhub")
    overheadDiscord.TextSize = 20

    local div = Instance.new("Frame")
    div.Name = "Divider"
    div.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 1)
    div.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -60, 0, 48)
    div.BackgroundColor3 = _G.PeakHubThemeAccent or Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 80)
    div.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    div.BorderSizePixel = 0
    div.Parent = bb

    overheadSpeed = makeLbl(52, 20, "Speed: " .. formatSpeedNum(Config.NormalSpeed or 59.5))
    overheadSpeed.TextSize = 15
end

function updateOverheadSpeedText()
    if overheadSpeed then
        overheadSpeed.Text = "Speed: " .. formatSpeedNum(getActiveSpeed())
        overheadSpeed.TextColor3 = _G.PeakHubThemeAccent or OVERHEAD_PURPLE
    end
end



overheadWasInRag = false
OVERHEAD_RAGDOLL_SECONDS = 2.6

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    ensureOverheadLabels(LP.Character)
    updateOverheadSpeedText()
    if overheadDiscord then overheadDiscord.TextColor3 = _G.PeakHubThemeAccent or OVERHEAD_PURPLE end
    if overheadSpeed then overheadSpeed.TextColor3 = _G.PeakHubThemeAccent or OVERHEAD_PURPLE end
    if overheadDiscord and overheadDiscord.Parent then
        local dividerObj = overheadDiscord.Parent:FindFirstChild("Divider")
        if dividerObj then dividerObj.BackgroundColor3 = _G.PeakHubThemeAccent or OVERHEAD_PURPLE end
    end

    local char = LP.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local inRag = false
    if hum then
        local st = hum:GetState()
        inRag = hum.PlatformStand
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
    end


    local endAttr = LP:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
    if type(endAttr) == "number" and endAttr > workspace:GetServerTimeNow() then
        inRag = true
    end



    if inRag and not overheadWasInRag then
        overheadRagEnd = tick() + OVERHEAD_RAGDOLL_SECONDS
    end
    overheadWasInRag = inRag

    if overheadRagdoll then
        local left = math.max(0, overheadRagEnd - tick())
        if Config.RagdollCountdown and overheadRagEnd > 0 and left > 0 then

            overheadRagdoll.Visible = true
            overheadRagdoll.Text = string.format("%.1f", left)
            overheadRagdoll.TextColor3 = _G.PeakHubThemeAccent or OVERHEAD_PURPLE
        else

            overheadRagdoll.Visible = false
            overheadRagdoll.Text = ""
            overheadRagEnd = 0
        end
    end
end)

overheadRespawnToken = 0
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    overheadRespawnToken += 1
    local thisToken = overheadRespawnToken
    task.spawn(function()
        local deadline = os.clock() + 8
        repeat
            if thisToken ~= overheadRespawnToken or LP.Character ~= char then return end
            ensureOverheadLabels(char)
            if overheadGui and overheadGui.Parent and overheadGui.Adornee
                and overheadGui.Adornee:IsDescendantOf(char) then
                return
            end
            task.wait(0.15)
        until os.clock() >= deadline
    end)
end)
if LP.Character then
    task.defer(function()
        local char = LP.Character
        for _ = 1, 20 do
            if LP.Character ~= char then return end
            ensureOverheadLabels(char)
            if overheadGui and overheadGui.Parent and overheadGui.Adornee
                and overheadGui.Adornee:IsDescendantOf(char) then
                return
            end
            task.wait(0.15)
        end
    end)
end




task.defer(function()
    task.wait(0.25)
    if Config.AntiRagdoll and _G._VyzeStartAntiRagdoll then
        pcall(_G._VyzeStartAntiRagdoll)
    end
    if syncAutoAntiDie then pcall(syncAutoAntiDie) end
    if Config.BatCounter and startBatCounter then pcall(startBatCounter) end
    if Config.MedusaCounter and startMedusaCounter then pcall(startMedusaCounter) end
    if Config.PlayerESP and startPlayerEsp then
        pcall(startPlayerEsp)
        Config.PlayerTracers = true
        if startPlayerTracers then pcall(startPlayerTracers) end
    elseif Config.PlayerTracers and startPlayerTracers then
        pcall(startPlayerTracers)
    end
    if Config.RagdollCountdown and LP.Character and hookRagdollCountdown then
        pcall(hookRagdollCountdown, LP.Character)
    end
    if Config.Unwalk and startUnwalk then pcall(startUnwalk) elseif Config.CustomAnim and applyAnimPack then pcall(applyAnimPack, Config.AnimPack) end
    if Config.NoCamCollision and setNoCamCollision then pcall(setNoCamCollision, true) end
    if Config.AutoTPDown then pcall(startAutoTPDown) end
    if Config.AntiLag and enableAntiLag then pcall(enableAntiLag) end
    if Config.StretchRes and enableStretchRez then pcall(enableStretchRez) end
    if Config.FOVChange and enableCustomFov then pcall(enableCustomFov) end
    if Config.AutoSteal and startAutoStealSystem then pcall(startAutoStealSystem) end
    if Config.BatAimbot and startBatAimbot then
        State.autoBatToggled = true
        pcall(startBatAimbot)
    end
end)



end
__vyzeInitVisualLogic()






do
    local LPl = LP
    local ALPHA_HELD = 0.85
    local ALPHA_BASE = 0.7
    local ATTR = "RHBrOrigT"
    local state = {
        held = Config.BrainrotTransparencyHeld == true,
        base = Config.BrainrotTransparencyBase == true,
        heldDirty = true,
        baseDirty = true,
    }

    local BODY = {
        Head=1, Torso=1, UpperTorso=1, LowerTorso=1, HumanoidRootPart=1,
        ["Left Arm"]=1, ["Right Arm"]=1, ["Left Leg"]=1, ["Right Leg"]=1,
        LeftUpperArm=1, LeftLowerArm=1, LeftHand=1, RightUpperArm=1, RightLowerArm=1, RightHand=1,
        LeftUpperLeg=1, LeftLowerLeg=1, LeftFoot=1, RightUpperLeg=1, RightLowerLeg=1, RightFoot=1,
    }

    local function isMedusaPiece(inst)
        local cur = inst
        while cur and cur ~= workspace and cur ~= game do
            if cur.Name == "RivalMedusaReplica" or cur.Name == "ShelMedusaBombSkin"
                or cur.Name:lower():find("medusa", 1, true) then
                return true
            end
            if cur:IsA("Tool") then
                local n = cur.Name:lower()
                if n:find("medusa", 1, true) or n:find("stone", 1, true) or n:find("head", 1, true) then
                    return true
                end
            end
            cur = cur.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    local function setT(inst, on, alpha)
        if inst:IsA("BasePart") or inst:IsA("Decal") or inst:IsA("Texture") then
            local o = inst:GetAttribute(ATTR)
            if isMedusaPiece(inst) then
                if o ~= nil then
                    inst.Transparency = o
                    inst:SetAttribute(ATTR, nil)
                end
                return
            end
            if on then
                if o == nil then o = inst.Transparency; inst:SetAttribute(ATTR, o) end
                local want = math.max(o, alpha)
                if inst.Transparency ~= want then inst.Transparency = want end
            elseif o ~= nil then
                inst.Transparency = o
                inst:SetAttribute(ATTR, nil)
            end
        end
    end

    local function setXray(root, on)
        local hl = root:FindFirstChild("RHBrXray")
        if on then
            if not hl then
                hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
                hl.Name = "RHBrXray"
                hl.Adornee = root
                hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
                hl.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 20)
                hl.FillTransparency = 1
                hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 130)
                hl.OutlineTransparency = 1
                hl.Parent = root
            else
                hl.FillTransparency = 1
                hl.OutlineTransparency = 1
            end
        elseif hl then
            hl:Destroy()
        end
    end

    local function applyTree(root, on, alpha)
        setT(root, on, alpha)
        for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do setT(d, on, alpha) end
        setXray(root, on)
    end

    local function topOf(part, char)
        local top = part
        local m = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        while m and m ~= workspace and m ~= char and not m:IsDescendantOf(char) do
            top = m
            m = m.Parent and m.Parent:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        end
        return top
    end

    local function heldTargets(char)
        local list, seen = {}, {}
        local function add(o) if o and not seen[o] then seen[o] = true; list[#list+1] = o end end
        for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Model") then
                add(child)
            elseif child:IsA("BasePart") and not BODY[child.Name] then
                add(child)
            end
        end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then
            local ok, connected = pcall(function() return root:GetConnectedParts(true) end)
            if ok and connected then
                for _, p in ipairs(connected) do
                    if not p:IsDescendantOf(char) then add(topOf(p, char)) end
                end
            end
        end
        return list
    end

    local function myPlot()
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return nil end
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            local yb = sign and sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
            if yb and yb:IsA("BillboardGui") and yb.Enabled then return plot end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local lastHeld = {}
    local function processHeld(on)
        local char = LPl.Character
        if not char then return end
        local targets = heldTargets(char)
        local current = {}
        for _, o in ipairs(targets) do
            current[o] = true
            applyTree(o, on, ALPHA_HELD)
            lastHeld[o] = true
        end
        for o in pairs(lastHeld) do
            if not current[o] then
                if o.Parent then applyTree(o, false, ALPHA_HELD) end
                lastHeld[o] = nil
            end
        end
    end

    local lastBase = {}
    local function baseTargets()
        local list, seen = {}, {}
        local function add(o) if o and not seen[o] then seen[o] = true; list[#list+1] = o end end
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return list end

        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if not pods then continue end

            local function isContainer(inst)
                return inst == workspace or inst == plot or inst == pods or inst.Parent == pods
                    or inst:IsA("Folder")
            end

            for _, pod in ipairs(pods:GetChildren()) do
                local podBase = pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
                for _, c in ipairs(pod:GetChildren()) do
                    if c ~= podBase and c:IsA("Model") and not c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then add(c) end
                end
                local spawnPart = podBase and podBase:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                if spawnPart and spawnPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local ok, parts = pcall(function()
                        return workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(spawnPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), 6)
                    end)
                    if ok and parts then
                        for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
                            if not part:IsDescendantOf(LPl.Character or workspace) or not LPl.Character then
                                local top = nil
                                local cur = part.Parent
                                while cur and not isContainer(cur) do
                                    if cur:IsA("Model") then top = cur end
                                    cur = cur.Parent
                                end
                                if top and top ~= pod and top ~= podBase and not top:IsAncestorOf(podBase)
                                   and not top:FindFirstChild("Spawn", true)
                                   and not top:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                   and not top:IsDescendantOf(podBase)
                                   and not (LPl.Character and top:IsDescendantOf(LPl.Character)) then
                                    add(top)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return list
    end

    local function processBase(on)
        local targets = baseTargets()
        local current = {}
        for _, o in ipairs(targets) do
            current[o] = true
            applyTree(o, on, ALPHA_BASE)
            lastBase[o] = true
        end
        for o in pairs(lastBase) do
            if not current[o] then
                if o.Parent then applyTree(o, false, ALPHA_BASE) end
                lastBase[o] = nil
            end
        end
    end

    local api = {
        SetHeld = function(v)
            state.held = v and true or false
            state.heldDirty = true
            Config.BrainrotTransparencyHeld = state.held
        end,
        SetBase = function(v)
            state.base = v and true or false
            state.baseDirty = true
            Config.BrainrotTransparencyBase = state.base
        end,
    }
    _G.RHBrainrotTransparency = api
    _G.PeakHubBrainrotTransparency = api

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            pcall(function()
                if state.held or state.heldDirty then processHeld(state.held); state.heldDirty = false end
            end)
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            pcall(function()
                if state.base or state.baseDirty then processBase(state.base); state.baseDirty = false end
            end)
            task.wait(0.4)
        end
    end)

    if Config.BrainrotTransparencyBase then api.SetBase(true) end
    if Config.BrainrotTransparencyHeld then api.SetHeld(true) end
end

function playVyzeIntro()
    if Config.IntroEnabled == false then return end

    local Tw = TweenService
    local ACCENT = _G.PeakHubThemeAccent or COLORS.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 0)
    local WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    local Soft = _G.PeakHubThemeSoft or COLORS.AccentSoft or Color3.fromRGB(100, 35, 0)

    local function tween(obj, time, props, style, dir)
        local t = Tw:Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(time, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props)
        t:Play()
        return t
    end

    local function round(obj, radius)
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
        c.CornerRadius = radius or UDim.new(1, 0)
        c.Parent = obj
        return c
    end

    local MUSIC_URL = Config.IntroMusicUrl or "https://files.catbox.moe/sdfi1j.mp3"
    local MUSIC_VOLUME = 0.6
    local MUSIC_START = 0

    local function resolveIntroMusicId(url)
        url = tostring(url or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if url == "" then return nil end
        local lower = string.lower(url)
        if lower:match("^rbxassetid://%d+$") then return url end
        if not (lower:sub(1, 8) == "https://" or lower:sub(1, 7) == "http://") then return nil end
        local fileName = "PeakHub_IntroMusic_" .. tostring(url):match("([^/]+)$"):gsub("[^%w%.%-_]", "_")
        local assetId = nil
        pcall(function()
            if isfile and isfile(fileName) then
                if getcustomasset then assetId = getcustomasset(fileName)
                elseif getsynasset then assetId = getsynasset(fileName) end
            end
        end)
        if assetId then return assetId end
        local data = nil
        pcall(function() data = game:HttpGet(url) end)
        if not data or #data < 100 then
            pcall(function()
                local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request)
                if not req then return end
                local res = req({Url = url, Method = "GET"})
                if res then data = res.Body or res.body end
            end)
        end
        if data and #data > 100 and writefile then
            pcall(function() writefile(fileName, data) end)
            pcall(function()
                if getcustomasset then assetId = getcustomasset(fileName)
                elseif getsynasset then assetId = getsynasset(fileName) end
            end)
        end
        return assetId
    end

    local musicId = nil
    local musicDone = false
    task.spawn(function()
        musicId = resolveIntroMusicId(MUSIC_URL)
        musicDone = true
    end)
    local waitStart = os.clock()
    while not musicDone and os.clock() - waitStart < 2 do
        task.wait()
    end

    local music
    if musicId then
        music = Instance.new("Sound")
        music.Name = "PeakHubIntroMusic"
        music.SoundId = musicId
        music.Volume = 0
        music.Looped = false
        music.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
        _G.PeakHubIntroMusic = music
        pcall(function()
            if music.TimeLength > MUSIC_START then music.TimePosition = MUSIC_START end
        end)
        music.Ended:Connect(function()
            pcall(function() music:Destroy() end)
        end)
        music:Play()
        tween(music, 0.8, { Volume = MUSIC_VOLUME }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
    end

    local blur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
    blur.Size = 0
    blur.Parent = Lighting
    tween(blur, 1, { Size = 28 })

    local pg = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    local old = pg:FindFirstChild("PeakHubIntro") or pg:FindFirstChild("VyzeIntro")
    if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "PeakHubIntro"
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.DisplayOrder = 9999
    gui.Parent = pg

    local introActive = true
    local running = true

    local bg = Instance.new("Frame")
    bg.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 6, 4)
    bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    bg.ClipsDescendants = true
    bg.Parent = gui

    local bgGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    bgGradient.Rotation = 90
    bgGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(8, 6, 4)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, Soft),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, ACCENT),
    })
    bgGradient.Parent = bg
    tween(bg, 0.8, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.05 })

    local rng = Random.new()
    for _ = 1, 70 do
        task.spawn(function()
            local s = Instance.new("Frame")
            local size = rng:NextInteger(2, 5)
            s.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
            s.BackgroundColor3 = (rng:NextNumber() > 0.5) and ACCENT or WHITE
            s.BorderSizePixel = 0
            s.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            s.Position = UDim2.fromScale(rng:NextNumber(), 1.05)
            s.ZIndex = 1
            s.Parent = bg
            round(s)
            task.wait(rng:NextNumber() * 3)
            while running and introActive and s.Parent do
                local dur = rng:NextNumber(3, 6)
                s.Position = UDim2.fromScale(rng:NextNumber(), 1.05)
                s.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                tween(s, dur * 0.2, { BackgroundTransparency = rng:NextNumber(0.2, 0.6) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
                local t = tween(s, dur, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(s.Position.X.Scale + rng:NextNumber(-0.05, 0.05), -0.05) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
                task.delay(dur * 0.8, function()
                    if s.Parent then tween(s, dur * 0.2, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine) end
                end)
                t.Completed:Wait()
            end
            if s then pcall(function() s:Destroy() end) end
        end)
    end

    local ring = Instance.new("Frame")
    ring.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    ring.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    ring.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ring.ZIndex = 2
    ring.Parent = bg
    round(ring)

    local ringStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    ringStroke.Thickness = 3
    ringStroke.Color = WHITE
    ringStroke.Transparency = 1
    ringStroke.Parent = ring

    local ringGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    ringGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(ACCENT, WHITE)
    ringGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, 0.1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
    })
    ringGradient.Parent = ringStroke

    local glow = Instance.new("Frame")
    glow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    glow.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    glow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    glow.BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT
    glow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    glow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    glow.ZIndex = 1
    glow.Parent = bg
    round(glow)

    local titleHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
    titleHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    titleHolder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    titleHolder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(520, 80)
    titleHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    titleHolder.ZIndex = 3
    titleHolder.Parent = bg

    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
    layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
    layout.Parent = titleHolder

    local letters = {}
    local titleText = "PEAK HUB"
    for i = 1, #titleText do
        local ch = titleText:sub(i, i)
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = ch
        l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        l.TextSize = 64
        l.TextColor3 = WHITE
        l.TextTransparency = 1
        l.Size = (ch == " ") and UDim2.fromOffset(22, 80) or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 80)
        if ch ~= " " then l.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X end
        l.LayoutOrder = i
        l.ZIndex = 3
        l.Parent = titleHolder
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, ACCENT),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, WHITE),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, ACCENT),
        })
        g.Rotation = 20
        g.Parent = l
        table.insert(letters, { label = l, gradient = g })
    end

    local sub = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    sub.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    sub.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    sub.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(500, 28)
    sub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    sub.Text = "R E A C H   T H E   T O P"
    sub.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    sub.TextSize = 16
    sub.TextColor3 = ACCENT:Lerp(WHITE, 0.35)
    sub.TextTransparency = 1
    sub.ZIndex = 3
    sub.Parent = bg

    local pct = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    pct.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    pct.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    pct.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 24)
    pct.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    pct.Text = "0%"
    pct.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    pct.TextSize = 18
    pct.TextColor3 = ACCENT
    pct.TextTransparency = 1
    pct.ZIndex = 3
    pct.Parent = bg

    local skipBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    skipBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    skipBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 18)
    skipBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(88, 36)
    skipBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 10, 8)
    skipBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    skipBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    skipBtn.Text = "SKIP"
    skipBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    skipBtn.TextSize = 14
    skipBtn.TextColor3 = WHITE
    skipBtn.ZIndex = 20
    skipBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    skipBtn.Parent = gui
    round(skipBtn, UDim.new(0, 8))
    local skipStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    skipStroke.Color = ACCENT
    skipStroke.Thickness = 1.4
    skipStroke.Transparency = 0.2
    skipStroke.Parent = skipBtn

    local spin
    local finishing = false

    local function finishIntro(fast)
        if finishing or not introActive then return end
        finishing = true
        introActive = false
        running = false
        if spin then pcall(function() spin:Cancel() end) end
        local d = fast and 0.35 or 0.55
        pcall(function()
            tween(glow, d, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1800, 1800), BackgroundTransparency = 0.7 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
            tween(ring, d, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1400, 1400) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
            tween(ringStroke, d, { Transparency = 1 })
            tween(sub, 0.3, { TextTransparency = 1 })
            tween(pct, 0.3, { TextTransparency = 1 })
            tween(skipBtn, 0.25, { TextTransparency = 1, BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
            for i, entry in ipairs(letters) do
                task.delay(i * 0.02, function()
                    if entry.label then
                        tween(entry.label, 0.4, { TextTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, -24) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
                    end
                end)
            end
            tween(blur, 0.6, { Size = 0 })
            tween(bg, 0.7, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
        end)
        task.delay(d + 0.35, function()
            pcall(function() if blur then blur:Destroy() end end)
            pcall(function() if gui then gui:Destroy() end end)
        end)
    end

    skipBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
        finishIntro(true)
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.15)
        if not introActive then return end

        tween(ring, 1.1, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 300) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
        tween(ringStroke, 0.8, { Transparency = 0 })
        tween(glow, 1.4, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(420, 420), BackgroundTransparency = 0.88 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)

        spin = Tw:Create(ringGradient, TweenInfo.new(1.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1), { Rotation = 360 })
        spin:Play()

        task.delay(1.4, function()
            if not introActive then return end
            local pulse = Tw:Create(glow, TweenInfo.new(1.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(480, 480),
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.93,
            })
            pulse:Play()
        end)

        task.wait(0.5)
        if not introActive then return end

        for i, entry in ipairs(letters) do
            local l = entry.label
            l.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 40)
            task.delay(i * 0.07, function()
                if not introActive then return end
                tween(l, 0.7, { TextTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
            end)
        end
        task.wait(#letters * 0.07 + 0.5)
        if not introActive then return end

        for _, entry in ipairs(letters) do
            entry.gradient.Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0)
        end
        local shineInfo = TweenInfo.new(1.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
        for i, entry in ipairs(letters) do
            task.delay(i * 0.05, function()
                if not introActive then return end
                Tw:Create(entry.gradient, shineInfo, { Offset = Vector2.new(1, 0) }):Play()
            end)
        end

        tween(titleHolder, 0.9, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.42) })
        task.wait(0.2)
        if not introActive then return end
        sub.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        tween(sub, 0.8, { TextTransparency = 0.1, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.51) })
        pct.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.6)
        tween(pct, 0.6, { TextTransparency = 0 })

        local value = Instance.new("NumberValue")
        value.Value = 0
        value.Changed:Connect(function(v)
            if pct and pct.Parent then pct.Text = math.floor(v) .. "%" end
        end)
        local pctTween = tween(value, 2.6, { Value = 100 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
        pctTween.Completed:Wait()
        if not introActive then return end
        task.wait(0.35)
        if not introActive then return end
        finishIntro(false)
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    pcall(playVyzeIntro)
end)


UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
    if keybindCaptureActive then return end
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
    if tostring(Config.HideUIKey or "") ~= "" and input.KeyCode.Name:upper() == tostring(Config.HideUIKey):upper() then
        if Config.HideUI or uiMinimized then
            restoreFromMini()
        else
            showMiniOnly()
            saveConfig()
        end
    end
end)

if Config.BatAimbot and not Config.SafeMode then
    startBatAimbot()
end


function peakMakeGuiClickable(obj)
    if not obj or not obj:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
    if obj:IsA("GuiButton") then
        obj.Active = true
        obj.Selectable = true
    elseif obj:IsA("TextBox") then
        obj.Active = true
        obj.Selectable = true
    elseif obj.Name == "UIBackground" or obj.Name == "UIBackgroundDim" then
        obj.Active = false
        obj.Selectable = false
    end
end
for _, guiObject in ipairs(screenGui:GetDescendants()) do peakMakeGuiClickable(guiObject) end
screenGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(guiObject)
    task.defer(function() pcall(peakMakeGuiClickable, guiObject) end)
end)
